# Noxa link inventory

Every link found on any captured Noxa page or social capture, one row per (page, href) pair.
Captured 2026-09-03.
1664 rows, 1071 distinct URLs, 368 rows whose target has its own file in this archive.

Type counts: external 425, internal 344, docs 251, asset 235, social 220, data 130, explorer 54, repo 3, chain 2.

`internal` is any noxa.fi, noxa.io, noxa.eth or noxacto.xyz URL; `docs` is docs.noxa.fi; `asset` is an IPFS gateway URL for a token logo; `external` is everything else, most of it site chrome on the DefiLlama capture.
